## 2026-10-21 РІР‚вЂќ Milestone 4: РЎвЂћР В°Р В·РЎвЂ№ Р В°РЎвЂљР В°Р С”Р С‘ Р С‘ Р В°Р Р…Р С‘Р СР С‘РЎР‚Р С•Р Р†Р В°Р Р…Р Р…РЎвЂ№Р в„– Р С—РЎР‚Р С•РЎвЂљР С‘Р Р†Р Р…Р С‘Р С”

- **Р В¤Р В°Р В·РЎвЂ№ Р В°РЎвЂљР В°Р С”Р С‘** Р Р† `MeleeAttack`: READY РІвЂ вЂ™ WINDUP РІвЂ вЂ™ ACTIVE РІвЂ вЂ™ RECOVERY. Р ТђР С‘РЎвЂљР В±Р С•Р С”РЎРѓ Р В·Р В°РЎРѓРЎвЂЎР С‘РЎвЂљРЎвЂ№Р Р†Р В°Р ВµРЎвЂљ Р С—Р С•Р С—Р В°Р Т‘Р В°Р Р…Р С‘РЎРЏ РЎвЂљР С•Р В»РЎРЉР С”Р С• Р Р† ACTIVE, Р С—Р С•-Р С—РЎР‚Р ВµР В¶Р Р…Р ВµР СРЎС“ Р Р…Р Вµ Р В±Р С•Р В»РЎРЉРЎв‚¬Р Вµ Р С•Р Т‘Р Р…Р С•Р С–Р С• Р С—Р С•Р С—Р В°Р Т‘Р В°Р Р…Р С‘РЎРЏ Р С—Р С• РЎвЂ Р ВµР В»Р С‘ Р В·Р В° Р В°РЎвЂљР В°Р С”РЎС“. Р СњР В°Р С—РЎР‚Р В°Р Р†Р В»Р ВµР Р…Р С‘Р Вµ РЎвЂћР С‘Р С”РЎРѓР С‘РЎР‚РЎС“Р ВµРЎвЂљРЎРѓРЎРЏ Р С—РЎР‚Р С‘ РЎРѓРЎвЂљР В°РЎР‚РЎвЂљР Вµ (РЎС“Р В¶Р Вµ Р Р…Р В° Р С—Р С•Р Т‘Р С–Р С•РЎвЂљР С•Р Р†Р С”Р Вµ). `disable()` Р С—РЎР‚Р С‘ РЎРѓР СР ВµРЎР‚РЎвЂљР С‘ Р С•Р В±РЎР‚РЎвЂ№Р Р†Р В°Р ВµРЎвЂљ Р В°РЎвЂљР В°Р С”РЎС“ Р Р† Р В»РЎР‹Р В±Р С•Р в„– РЎвЂћР В°Р В·Р Вµ. Р вЂ™Р С•РЎРѓРЎРѓРЎвЂљР В°Р Р…Р С•Р Р†Р В»Р ВµР Р…Р С‘Р Вµ РІР‚вЂќ Р С•РЎРѓРЎвЂљР В°РЎвЂљР С•Р С” `cooldown` Р С—Р С•РЎРѓР В»Р Вµ Р С•Р С”Р Р…Р В°, Р С—Р С•РЎРЊРЎвЂљР С•Р СРЎС“ Р Т‘Р В»Р С‘Р Р…Р В° РЎвЂ Р С‘Р С”Р В»Р В° Р В°РЎвЂљР В°Р С”Р С‘ Р Р…Р Вµ Р С‘Р В·Р СР ВµР Р…Р С‘Р В»Р В°РЎРѓРЎРЉ. Р РЋР С‘Р С–Р Р…Р В°Р В»РЎвЂ№: `attack_started`, `hit_window_opened`, `hit_window_closed`, `attack_finished`, `hit_landed`.
- **Р СћР В°Р в„–Р СР С‘Р Р…Р С–Р С‘ Р С—Р С• РЎР‚Р ВµР В°Р В»РЎРЉР Р…РЎвЂ№Р С Р С”Р В°Р Т‘РЎР‚Р В°Р С.** Р СљР С•Р СР ВµР Р…РЎвЂљ Р С”Р С•Р Р…РЎвЂљР В°Р С”РЎвЂљР В° Р С‘Р В·Р СР ВµРЎР‚Р ВµР Р… Р С—Р С•Р С”Р В°Р Т‘РЎР‚Р С•Р Р†Р С• (1/60 РЎРѓ) Р С—Р С• Р С—Р С•Р В·Р Вµ РЎРѓР С”Р ВµР В»Р ВµРЎвЂљР В°: РЎРѓР С”Р С•РЎР‚Р С•РЎРѓРЎвЂљРЎРЉ Р С”Р С•РЎРѓРЎвЂљР С‘ `handslot.r` Р С—Р ВµРЎР‚Р ВµР Т‘ Р С”Р С•РЎР‚Р С—РЎС“РЎРѓР С•Р С. Р РЋР В°Р Р…Р Р…Р С‘, `1H_Melee_Attack_Slice_Horizontal`: Р В·Р В°Р СР В°РЎвЂ¦ 0РІР‚вЂњ0,20 РЎРѓ, Р С”Р С•Р Р…РЎвЂљР В°Р С”РЎвЂљ 0,25 РЎРѓ, Р Т‘Р С•Р Р†Р С•Р Т‘Р С”Р В° Р Т‘Р С• 0,35 РЎРѓ РІвЂ вЂ™ `windup_time = 0.2` (Р Р…Р С•Р Р†РЎвЂ№Р в„– Р С—Р В°РЎР‚Р В°Р СР ВµРЎвЂљРЎР‚ `AttackStats`), `active_time = 0.15` Р С‘ `cooldown = 0.5` Р В±Р ВµР В· Р С‘Р В·Р СР ВµР Р…Р ВµР Р…Р С‘Р в„–. Р вЂєР С•Р С”Р В°РЎвЂ Р С‘Р С‘ РІР‚вЂќ Р Р…Р В°РЎРѓР В»Р ВµР Т‘Р Р…Р С‘Р С”Р С‘ `SliceLocation` РЎРѓ Р ВµР Т‘Р С‘Р Р…РЎвЂ№Р С Р С‘Р Р…РЎвЂљР ВµРЎР‚РЎвЂћР ВµР в„–РЎРѓР С•Р С: `Spawns/*`, `Shots/*` (РЎР‚Р В°Р С”РЎС“РЎР‚РЎРѓ РЎРѓР СР С•РЎвЂљРЎР‚Р С‘РЎвЂљ Р Р…Р В° Р Т‘Р С•РЎвЂЎР ВµРЎР‚Р Р…Р С‘Р в„– `LookAt`), `Interactable`, `TriggerZone`, `play_cutscene(id, fast)`, `march_velocity()`. - **Р СџР ВµРЎР‚РЎРѓР С•Р Р…Р В°Р В¶ Р Р† Р С—Р С•РЎРѓРЎвЂљР В°Р Р…Р С•Р Р†Р С”Р В°РЎвЂ¦:** `CinematicActor` + `ActionClipMap` (`resources/slice/sunny_kaykit_clips.tres`) Р С—Р ВµРЎР‚Р ВµР Р†Р С•Р Т‘РЎРЏРЎвЂљ Р В»Р С•Р С–Р С‘РЎвЂЎР ВµРЎРѓР С”Р С‘Р Вµ Р Т‘Р ВµР в„–РЎРѓРЎвЂљР Р†Р С‘РЎРЏ (Р’В«РЎРѓР ВµРЎРѓРЎвЂљРЎРЉР’В», Р’В«Р Р†РЎРѓРЎвЂљР В°РЎвЂљРЎРЉР’В», Р’В«Р В±РЎР‚Р С•РЎРѓР С‘РЎвЂљРЎРЉР’В») Р Р† Р С‘Р СР ВµР Р…Р В° Р С”Р В»Р С‘Р С—Р С•Р Р†. Р СњР В° Р Р†РЎР‚Р ВµР СРЎРЏ Р С—Р С•РЎРѓРЎвЂљР В°Р Р…Р С•Р Р†Р С”Р С‘ Р В°Р С”РЎвЂљРЎвЂРЎР‚ Р Р†Р С”Р В»РЎР‹РЎвЂЎР В°Р ВµРЎвЂљ РЎС“ `SunnyAnimator` РЎР‚Р ВµР В¶Р С‘Р С `cinematic`, РЎвЂЎРЎвЂљР С•Р В±РЎвЂ№ Р Т‘Р Р†Р В° Р С‘РЎРѓРЎвЂљР С•РЎвЂЎР Р…Р С‘Р С”Р В° Р Р…Р Вµ Р Т‘Р ВµР В»Р С‘Р В»Р С‘ Р С”Р С•РЎРѓРЎвЂљР С‘.

Р СџР С•РЎРѓРЎвЂљР В°Р Р…Р С•Р Р†Р С”Р С‘ Р Т‘Р Р†Р С‘Р С–Р В°РЎР‹РЎвЂљ РЎС“Р В·Р ВµР В» Р СР В°Р Р…Р ВµР С”Р ВµР Р…Р В°, Р В° Р Р…Р Вµ Р СР ВµРЎв‚¬Р С‘, Р С—Р С•РЎРЊРЎвЂљР С•Р СРЎС“ Р В·Р В°Р СР ВµР Р…Р В° Р Р…Р В° Р СР С•Р Т‘Р ВµР В»РЎРЉ Р Р…Р Вµ Р СР ВµР Р…РЎРЏР ВµРЎвЂљ РЎРѓРЎвЂ Р ВµР Р…Р В°РЎР‚Р С‘Р в„–. Р СњР С•Р Р†РЎвЂ№РЎвЂ¦ Р Р†Р Р…Р ВµРЎв‚¬Р Р…Р С‘РЎвЂ¦ Р В°РЎРѓРЎРѓР ВµРЎвЂљР С•Р Р† Р Р…Р ВµРЎвЂљ.

## 2026-10-31 РІР‚вЂќ Р СџРЎР‚Р С•РЎвЂљР С•РЎвЂљР С‘Р С—РЎвЂ№ Р В»Р С•Р С”Р В°РЎвЂ Р С‘Р в„– Р С—Р С•РЎРѓР В»Р Вµ Р СџР ВµРЎР‚Р Р†Р С•Р С–Р С• Р С™Р С•РЎв‚¬Р СР В°РЎР‚Р В°

- **Р С›Р В±Р В»Р В°РЎРѓРЎвЂљРЎРЉ.** Р СџРЎРЏРЎвЂљРЎРЉ greybox-Р В»Р С•Р С”Р В°РЎвЂ Р С‘Р в„– Р С—Р С• Р С”Р В°Р Р…Р С•Р Р…РЎС“ Р С–Р В»Р В°Р Р† 17РІР‚вЂњ95: Р С—Р С•Р В»Р С‘РЎвЂ Р ВµР в„–РЎРѓР С”Р С‘Р в„– РЎС“РЎвЂЎР В°РЎРѓРЎвЂљР С•Р С” (`police_precinct`, P01РІР‚вЂњP02), Р Р†Р С•РЎР‚Р С•РЎвЂљР В° Р С’Р С”Р В°Р Т‘Р ВµР СР С‘Р С‘ (`academy_gate`, P03), РЎРѓР С—Р В°Р В»РЎРЉР Р…РЎвЂ№Р в„– РЎвЂ Р ВµР Р…РЎвЂљРЎР‚ (`academy_sleeper_center`, P04РІР‚вЂњP06), Р С—Р В»Р В°РЎвЂљРЎвЂћР С•РЎР‚Р СР В° Р В§РЎвЂРЎР‚Р Р…Р С•Р С–Р С• Р СР С•РЎР‚РЎРЏ (`black_sea_platform`, B01РІР‚вЂњB02) Р С‘ Р С”Р С•РЎР‚Р В°Р В»Р В»Р С•Р Р†РЎвЂ№Р в„– Р В»Р В°Р В±Р С‘РЎР‚Р С‘Р Р…РЎвЂљ (`coral_labyrinth`, B03РІР‚вЂњB05). Р В­РЎвЂљР С• РЎв‚¬Р В°Р В±Р В»Р С•Р Р…РЎвЂ№ Р Т‘Р В»РЎРЏ Р Т‘Р В°Р В»РЎРЉР Р…Р ВµР в„–РЎв‚¬Р ВµР в„– Р Т‘Р ВµРЎвЂљР В°Р В»Р С‘Р В·Р В°РЎвЂ Р С‘Р С‘, Р В° Р Р…Р Вµ РЎвЂћР С‘Р Р…Р В°Р В»РЎРЉР Р…РЎвЂ№Р в„– Р В°РЎР‚РЎвЂљ.
- **Р С’РЎР‚РЎвЂ¦Р С‘РЎвЂљР ВµР С”РЎвЂљРЎС“РЎР‚Р В°.** Р С›Р В±РЎвЂ°Р С‘Р в„– runtime-РЎРѓРЎвЂљРЎР‚Р С•Р С‘РЎвЂљР ВµР В»РЎРЉ `GreyboxLocation` РЎРѓР С•Р В±Р С‘РЎР‚Р В°Р ВµРЎвЂљ Р С”Р С•Р СР Р…Р В°РЎвЂљРЎвЂ№, Р С”Р С•РЎР‚Р С‘Р Т‘Р С•РЎР‚РЎвЂ№, Р С—Р В»Р С•РЎвЂ°Р В°Р Т‘Р С”Р С‘, Р В»Р ВµРЎРѓРЎвЂљР Р…Р С‘РЎвЂ РЎвЂ№, Р С•Р С–РЎР‚Р В°Р В¶Р Т‘Р ВµР Р…Р С‘РЎРЏ Р С‘ РЎР‚Р ВµР С”Р Р†Р С‘Р В·Р С‘РЎвЂљ Р С‘Р В· Р С—РЎР‚Р С‘Р СР С‘РЎвЂљР С‘Р Р†Р С•Р Р†; РЎС“ Р С”Р В°Р В¶Р Т‘Р С•Р в„– Р В»Р С•Р С”Р В°РЎвЂ Р С‘Р С‘ РЎРѓР Р†Р С•Р в„– РЎРѓР С”РЎР‚Р С‘Р С—РЎвЂљ РЎРѓ Р С—Р В»Р В°Р Р…Р С‘РЎР‚Р С•Р Р†Р С”Р С•Р в„–. Р вЂњР ВµР С•Р СР ВµРЎвЂљРЎР‚Р С‘РЎРЏ РЎРѓР С”Р В»Р В°Р Т‘РЎвЂ№Р Р†Р В°Р ВµРЎвЂљРЎРѓРЎРЏ Р Р† `Generated/Structure`, `Generated/Props`, `Generated/Lights`; `rebuild()` Р С—Р С•Р Р†РЎвЂљР С•РЎР‚РЎРЏР ВµР С Р С‘ Р Р…Р Вµ РЎвЂљРЎР‚Р С•Р С–Р В°Р ВµРЎвЂљ Р В°Р Р†РЎвЂљР С•РЎР‚РЎРѓР С”Р С‘Р Вµ РЎС“Р В·Р В»РЎвЂ№ РЎРѓРЎвЂ Р ВµР Р…РЎвЂ№.

Р В­РЎвЂљР С• Р С—Р С•Р В·Р Р†Р С•Р В»РЎРЏР ВµРЎвЂљ Р СР ВµР Р…РЎРЏРЎвЂљРЎРЉ greybox, Р Р…Р Вµ Р В»Р С•Р СР В°РЎРЏ РЎвЂљР С•РЎвЂЎР С”Р С‘ Р С—Р ВµРЎР‚Р ВµРЎвЂ¦Р С•Р Т‘Р В°, РЎР‚Р В°Р С”РЎС“РЎР‚РЎРѓРЎвЂ№ Р С‘ Р С”Р В°РЎР‚РЎвЂљРЎС“.
- **Р В Р ВµРЎРѓРЎС“РЎР‚РЎРѓРЎвЂ№.** Р С›Р В±РЎвЂ°Р С‘Р Вµ Р СР В°РЎвЂљР ВµРЎР‚Р С‘Р В°Р В»РЎвЂ№ Р С‘ Р С•Р С”РЎР‚РЎС“Р В¶Р ВµР Р…Р С‘РЎРЏ Р В»Р ВµР В¶Р В°РЎвЂљ Р Р† `resources/locations/post_nightmare/`; Р Т‘Р В»РЎРЏ Р С”РЎР‚РЎС“Р С—Р Р…РЎвЂ№РЎвЂ¦ РЎРЊР С”РЎР‚Р В°Р Р…Р С•Р Р† Р Т‘Р С•Р В±Р В°Р Р†Р В»Р ВµР Р… Р С—РЎР‚Р С‘Р С–Р В»РЎС“РЎв‚¬РЎвЂР Р…Р Р…РЎвЂ№Р в„– `screen_cyan_dim`, РЎвЂЎРЎвЂљР С•Р В±РЎвЂ№ Р Р…Р Вµ Р Р†РЎвЂ№Р В¶Р С‘Р С–Р В°РЎвЂљРЎРЉ Р С”Р В°Р Т‘РЎР‚. - **Р СџРЎР‚Р С•Р Р†Р ВµРЎР‚Р С”Р В°.** Р СњР С•Р Р†РЎвЂ№Р Вµ РЎвЂљР ВµРЎРѓРЎвЂљРЎвЂ№: `tests/test_campaign_guide.gd` (8), `tests/test_guide_beacon.gd` (3, Р Р†Р С”Р В»РЎР‹РЎвЂЎР В°РЎРЏ Р В°РЎР‚РЎвЂљ-Р Р†Р В°РЎР‚Р С‘Р В°Р Р…РЎвЂљ РЎвЂљРЎР‚Р С•Р С—РЎвЂ№). ## 2026-11-01 РІР‚вЂќ Р С’РЎС“Р Т‘Р С‘Р С•: Р В±Р В°Р В·Р С•Р Р†РЎвЂ№Р в„– Р С”Р В°РЎР‚Р С”Р В°РЎРѓ AudioDirector (1.4, Р С—РЎС“Р Р…Р С”РЎвЂљРЎвЂ№ 1РІР‚вЂњ5)

- **Р РЃР С‘Р Р…РЎвЂ№.** `default_bus_layout.tres` РЎРѓР С•Р В·Р Т‘Р В°РЎвЂРЎвЂљ `Master`, `Music`, `Ambience`, `SFX`, `Voice`, `UI`, `Reverb`. `Music` Р С‘ `Ambience` Р С•РЎвЂљР С—РЎР‚Р В°Р Р†Р В»РЎРЏРЎР‹РЎвЂљРЎРѓРЎРЏ Р Р† `Reverb`, Р С•РЎРѓРЎвЂљР В°Р В»РЎРЉР Р…РЎвЂ№Р Вµ РІР‚вЂќ Р Р…Р В°Р С—РЎР‚РЎРЏР СРЎС“РЎР‹ Р Р† `Master`; Р Р†Р С•Р В·Р Р†РЎР‚Р В°РЎвЂљ `Reverb` Р С‘Р Т‘РЎвЂРЎвЂљ Р Р† `Master` РЎРѓ `wet=0.25`, `dry=1.0`. - **Autoload.** `AudioDirector` (`scripts/audio/audio_director.gd`) РІР‚вЂќ Р ВµР Т‘Р С‘Р Р…РЎРѓРЎвЂљР Р†Р ВµР Р…Р Р…Р В°РЎРЏ РЎвЂљР С•РЎвЂЎР С”Р В° Р Р†РЎвЂ¦Р С•Р Т‘Р В° Р Т‘Р В»РЎРЏ РЎРѓРЎвЂ Р ВµР Р…. ## 2026-11-02 ? P3 First Nightmare: art-overlay, VFX, cameras

- **P3 scope.** L01?L16 / `finale_arrival` visual pass only. Base routes, collision, controllers, interactables and shared systems are untouched. New dressing is a detachable art layer.
- **Road/site/trail/vision/sanctuary** use `p3_*_dressing.tscn` scenes with snow gust, breath, smoke, dust, footprints/blood/chain marks and volumetric light shafts. Existing GLB kits remain the geometry source.
- **Surface decals.** Footprint/chain/blood marks follow the road incline (`GroundDetails` copies the road transform) and use thin unshaded mesh quads above the surface, because flat world-space decals were hidden by the elevated road meshes.
- **Camera repair.** `NightMontageCamera` moved to `Vector3(4.6, 3.4, 4.6)` with `look_at` on the camp fire; `WagonCamera` moved to `Vector3(4.5, 3.0, 1.2)` with `look_at` on the wagon. Both changes are visual-only and are captured through the real campaign camera methods.
- **Vision caravan.** The caravan remains controlled by the existing `MountainVision._vision` cutscene; the audit capture enables it explicitly for evidence and does not force it visible during normal play.
- **Marble.** Sanctuary adds a polished central marble strip (`p3_marble_polished.tres`, roughness 0.42) plus a ReflectionProbe and volumetric beams. It reuses the existing project-imported black-marble textures.
- **Checks.** Import exit 0; P3/P2.5 scene validation 20/20; selected GUT: `test_location_art_decor` 3/3, `test_caravan_march` 7/7, `test_caravan_site` 7/7, `test_pilgrim_path` 10/10, `test_sanctuary_approach_presentation` 10/10, `test_shadow_sanctuary` 11/11, `test_slice_locations` 8/8, `test_campaign_flow` 10/10. Full GUT: 71 scripts, 557 tests, 557 pass, 6761 asserts, exit 0. Manual playthrough, foot-sliding and 1080p/60 performance are **NOT TESTED**.
- **Report.** `docs/art/p3_first_nightmare.md`; previews in `docs/art/previews/p3_first_nightmare/`.


## 2026-11-02 ? P2.5 Spell Void: ??????? ? ?????? ??????

- **Scope.** P2.5 is treated as the previously empty **L04 / B13 `spell_void`** bridge between P2 (real world) and P3 (First Nightmare). The old scene was a black placeholder; it now contains the full visual transition. P3 routes, combat, collisions and checkpoints are untouched.
- **Visuals.** `spell_void.tscn` adds a black reflective floor, three emissive rune rings, eight rune marks, a floating Sunny silhouette, three particle layers, volumetric fog, cold key/rim lights and a slow camera push. `spell_void.gd` animates the circle, body and camera; `p25_void_motes.tscn` owns the reusable void particles.
- **Integration.** `SliceDirector` B13 now fades from black into the void, calls `play_cutscene("spell")`, then shows the existing Spell message and continues to B14 mountain vision. The transition is skipped instantly in `fast_mode` for tests.
- **Checks.** Import exit 0; P3/P2.5 validation 20/20; selected `test_slice_locations` 8/8, `test_campaign_flow` 10/10 and `test_spell_void_transition` 3/3; full GUT 71 scripts, 557 tests, 6761 asserts, exit 0. Captures at 1280?720 in `docs/art/previews/p2_5_spell_void/`.
- **NOT TESTED.** Manual playthrough of the transition, subjective pacing on a real monitor, 1080p/60 performance. No new external assets or licenses were introduced.

## 2026-11-02 ? P4 UI and staging

- **Debug UI.** Removed `PrototypeNote` and its script references from the three campaign wrappers, removed the `Prototype` signature from the finale, removed `PROTOTYPE BUILD` and the RPG-menu debug path/footer, and disabled post-nightmare greybox `show_debug_labels`. Gameplay flags and debug direct-scene entry logic remain intact; only visible text was removed. Tests that asserted the old notes now assert their absence.
- **Unified UI.** `RpgMenuTheme` is the single visual source for campaign UI: warm graphite, bronze/gold accents and bone text. `SubtitleUI`, `ObjectiveLine`, `RuneStatusUI`, `CombatHud` and the `PrologueUI` labels apply it; campaign cards were recolored to match. `SpellMessage` remains cold blue as the semantic voice of the Nightmare Spell.
- **Cameras.** `AlarmShot` now frames the lobby desk head-on at `(0, 2.15, 4.2)` with target `(0, 1.3, -3.2)` and FOV 54. P3's `WagonCamera` and `NightMontageCamera` positions were retained and revalidated through their real staging methods. All P2/P2.5/P3 `Shots/*` cameras were audited.
- **DOF.** `resources/cameras/p4_cutscene_dof.tres` is assigned by `SliceLocation` to every `Shots/*` camera and explicitly to `WagonCamera`/`NightMontageCamera`. The player's third-person camera has no `CameraAttributes`; DOF never leaks into gameplay.
- **Color correction.** `resources/environment/p4_grade_lut.tres` is a project-owned 1D LUT (GradientTexture1D) with a soft split-tone. It is assigned to the P2 lobby/cell/park environments and the P3 road/site/pilgrim/sanctuary/mountain/spell-void environments; no textures or third-party assets were added.
- **Checks.** Import exit 0; P3/P2.5 validation 20/20; full GUT 72 scripts, 567 tests, 567 pass, 6825 asserts, exit 0. Captures: `docs/art/previews/p4_staging/`; report: `docs/art/p4_ui_staging.md`. Manual playthrough, 1080p/60 and subjective DOF/LOOK pacing are **NOT TESTED**.

## 2026-11-02 ? P5 optimization and QA

- **Scope.** P5 targets the realistic park because it is the first location where facade, tree and distant-city draw calls overlap. Gameplay routes, collisions, P2-P4 compositions and hero-character count are unchanged.
- **Imported geometry.** Five park P2 GLBs (stone/balcony/glass facades, station entrance, bus shelter) were copied to `assets/environments/park/p2/p5/` and passed through `gltf-transform join`; the originals remain untouched. `p2_park_set.tscn` points at the joined copies. The join reduces many small facade MeshInstance3D primitives to a small number of material-compatible primitives.
- **Batching and culling.** `scripts/world/park_batch.gd` converts repeated `DistantCity` and `Upgrade` static props to `MultiMeshInstance3D` at runtime while hiding, not deleting, the authored nodes. `p2_park_set.tscn` adds a station `BoxOccluder3D` via `OccluderInstance3D`; `project.godot` enables occlusion culling, S3TC/BPTC VRAM compression and mesh LOD pixel threshold 1.5. The existing `island_tree.tscn`/`tree_lod.gd` three-level tree LOD remains the tree path.
- **Measured park result.** 1920x1080, real Forward+ run, fixed bench camera: draw calls 1,104.8 -> 251.8; primitives 1.88M -> 1.566M; objects 1,472.8 -> 327.8; VRAM 1,068.6 MB; uncapped ~165 FPS (~6.1 ms). Four static camera angles gave 211-388 draw calls; the actual prologue with player + UI measured 709.0 draw calls, 1.86M primitives and 1,078.1 MB VRAM. Targets: <1,500 draw calls, <4 GB VRAM, >=60 FPS at 1080p.
- **Checks.** Import exit 0; P5 tests 5/5 (23 asserts); full GUT 73 scripts, 572 tests, 572 pass, 6848 asserts, exit 0. Benchmark: `tools/audit/p5_benchmark.gd`; report: `docs/art/p5_optimization.md`.
- **NOT TESTED.** Manual gameplay with vsync, subjective LOD-pop review while moving, thermal/VRAM soak, and a second GPU. The FPS result is an uncapped benchmark, not a guarantee of stable 60 FPS everywhere.
- **Audit finding.** The first `P5Batch` pass checked only `MeshInstance3D.visible`; hidden legacy `Tree0-Tree4` and `Facade0-Facade7` roots still have visible child meshes, so they were batched as dark overlays. The batcher now uses `is_visible_in_tree()`; the final park capture is clean. It also uses a tight per-batch AABB instead of one oversized AABB, so culling can drop the batches outside the view.
- **Theme cache.** The audited P4 UI paths rebuilt `RpgMenuTheme` independently in subtitles, objective, rune status, combat HUD and labels. `RpgMenuTheme.build()` now returns one cached immutable theme, removing duplicate font/style allocation; menu, HUD, P4 and P5 tests pass.

## 2026-11-02 РІР‚вЂќ Enemy model audit and upgrade

- **Scope.** The active canon enemies are the Mountain King's Larva and the Mountain King. `Barbarian.glb` remains a test-arena placeholder (KayKit, 41 bones, chibi style), not a canon enemy. `ss_shade_*` in `assets/environments/soul_sea/kit/` are static environment silhouettes, not combat actors; they keep their silhouette-first, no-rig contract.
- **Larva.** `scenes/locations/caravan_site/larva.tscn` now instances `assets/characters/enemies/larva/larva_corpse.glb` instead of CSG primitives. Source: Sketchfab Р’В«Bloodspike Horror CreatureР’В» by Purple.Point, CC BY 4.0; decimated 261 689 РІвЂ вЂ™ 28 785 tri, 25-bone rig and Idle clip kept, scaled to ~1.03 m, normal/roughness maps generated and albedo regraded. `larva.gd` plays the Idle clip; collision capsule, hurtbox, weak spot and melee origin were aligned to the new silhouette.
- **Mountain King.** `assets/characters/p1/models/mountain_king_p2.glb` is a visual derivative of `mountain_king_p1.glb`: fur rebuilt as curved matted tufts, all rigid parts joined into three LOD meshes (LOD0 42 256 / LOD1 19 436 / LOD2 7 567 tri; 6 materials), while the 25-bone rig and the five clips (Idle, Walk, Telegraph, Attack, Death) are unchanged. `scenes/characters/mountain_king.tscn` points to p2; p1 remains in the repository as a fallback. The source pass is `assets/characters/p1/source/upgrade_mountain_king_v2.py`.
- **Licensing.** The larva asset and its derived maps are registered in `docs/ASSET_LICENSES.md`; CC BY 4.0 attribution must be present in release credits. The Mountain King p2 is project-owned, derived from the project's p1 asset.
- **Checks.** Headless import exit 0; `test_mountain_king`, `test_caravan_site`, `test_larva_enemy`, `test_ambush_site` and `test_pilgrim_path` 49/49, 963 asserts; Godot previews confirm the larva textures/rig and the Mountain King LOD0/1/2 + five clips. Full manual playthrough, live combat readability and foot/IK polish are **NOT TESTED**.

## 2026-11-02 РІР‚вЂќ Enemy follow-up: Mountain King p3, larva LOD, carapace AI

- **Mountain King p3.** `mountain_king.tscn` now points to `mountain_king_p3.glb`: short arms pulled out of the fur with weight falloff, face strengthened (brow ridges, cheekbones, muzzle bridge, lips, chin, jaw corners, tusks), dark neck mane added. The 25-bone rig and five clips are unchanged. LOD0/1/2 = 46 148 / 21 228 / 8 267 tri. p1/p2 remain fallbacks. Source: `assets/characters/p1/source/upgrade_mountain_king_v3.py`.
- **Larva LOD.** `larva_corpse_lod.glb`: LOD0 28 785, LOD1 14 391, LOD2 6 332 tri, same 25-bone rig and `Idle`. `larva.tscn` adds `CharacterLodVisibility` (8 m / 16 m); `larva_corpse.glb` remains fallback.
- **Carapace Scavenger.** New project-owned `carapace_scavenger.glb` (31 bones, 5 clips, LOD0/1/2 = 5 940 / 2 726 / 1 034 tri) and `scenes/enemies/carapace_enemy.tscn`. `CarapaceEnemy` is a separate AI, not a `TestEnemy` subclass: sideways scuttle, slow turn, longer telegraph, claw snap with a short forward lunge, 95 HP / 18 damage. `combat_arena.tscn` now instances the carapace; the Barbarian test scene, assets and tests remain as fallback.
- **Contract.** `combat_arena.gd` now uses the shared `health`/HUD contract instead of a hard `TestEnemy` type, so the arena accepts LarvaEnemy and CarapaceEnemy. `test_combat_arena` was updated to `CarapaceEnemy`; `test_carapace_enemy` covers data, scuttle direction, separate AI cycle, death and five clips.
- **Checks.** Headless import exit 0; selected suite 92/92, 1381 asserts (including `test_mountain_king` 11/11, `test_ambush_site` 16/16, `test_caravan_site` 7/7, `test_larva_enemy` 5/5, `test_carapace_enemy` 5/5, `test_combat_arena` 6/6, `test_full_route` 3/3). Full GUT, manual fight/scuttle and 1080p/60 РІР‚вЂќ **NOT TESTED**.
- **Docs.** `docs/art/enemy_model_upgrade_followup_2026-11-02.md`; previews in `docs/art/previews/enemies/`. No new external assets/licenses.

## 2026-11-02 РІР‚вЂќ Character rework blocked on Hyper3D credentials

- **User verdict:** the cast gear v2 pass is unacceptable (floating pauldrons/bracers, rigid skirt, narrow silhouettes); the underlying P1 bodies/clothing and UAL deformation are also not final quality.
- **Rollback:** `scripts/slice/mannequin.gd` now uses the original P1 assets for hero/auro, veteran, shifty, slave/mass_slave and knight/mass_soldier. v2 GLBs remain fallback only. Extreme `width_scale` values (0.9, 1.4/1.35) were reduced. Tests after rollback: 32/32, 871 asserts.
- **Generator recheck:** Hyper3D free-trial key returns `API_INSUFFICIENT_FUNDS`; the saved private key (64 chars) returns `API_NO_ACTIVE_SUBSCRIPTION`; FAL_AI requires its own authentication; Hunyuan3D/Meshy/Tripo are off and unconfigured; no MCP Premium license is set. No generation was produced or charged.
- **Next:** activate/pay for Hyper3D or supply a working key, or approve a stock/MPFB fallback. Details and options: `docs/art/character_rework_status_2026-11-02.md`.
- **Incident:** diagnostics reset the open Blender scene to an empty file; project assets are untouched. Autosave: `C:\Users\user\AppData\Local\Temp\55564_autosave.blend`.

## 2026-11-02 РІР‚вЂќ Generated-character intake queue

- **Decision:** Hyper3D API remains unusable on the current Creator account (`API_NO_ACTIVE_SUBSCRIPTION` on the current `api.hyper3d.com` endpoint, all tiers). The user will generate the required characters/monsters in the Hyper3D **web UI**; the project will not pretend API generation works.
- **Intake:** `incoming_models/` with `.gdignore` and `P0_characters`, `P1_monsters`, `P1_police`, `P2_crowd`, `P2_environment`, `_rejected`. Files use exact names from `docs/art/generation_queue_2026-11-02.md`.
- **Priority:** P0 = Sunny real/nightmare, Hero/Auro, Shifty, Scholar, Veteran, BroadSlave, white horse, Mountain King. P1 = Carapace Scavenger/Centurion, Centipede, Worm Knot, Blood Flower, optional police. P2 crowd/environment only after P0/P1 acceptance.
- **Do not generate now:** Larva (already good), coral kit (60 GLB), Soul Sea kit (63 GLB), park/lobby/cell props, found Stone Knight statue, Nephis/Cassia/Echo (markers only, no design/gameplay).
- **Audit:** `docs/art/character_asset_audit_2026-11-02.md` records current GLB stats (P1 double-sided/no tangents, Mountain King textureless, larva heavy, carapace textureless before v2). New generated models replace, not duplicate, current fallbacks.

## 2026-11-02 РІР‚вЂќ Hyper3D CLI pipeline and generated rig findings

- **CLI works.** `@hyper3d/cli 0.3.0` authenticated via OAuth; generation costs 0.5 subscription credits; rigging costs 0.5. This bypasses the API-key Creator limitation for both generation and rigging.
- **Generation settings:** use `Gen-2.5-High`, `Quad --quality 50000` for humanoids, `Raw --quality 150000` for complex monsters, `--format glb`, PBR. Text prompts are capped at 1024 characters.
- **Rigging:** `--bone-template human` works and returns a 52-bone Mixamo-style skeleton. `--bone-template ue` is rejected by the backend (`data/bone_template` invalid) despite being advertised in the CLI schema; do not use it for production until fixed.
- **Skinning:** `--skinning-method shell` can explode layered clothing (Sunny sweater shards). `core` fixed Sunny. Still, generated meshes are not clean: Sunny raw has 526 disconnected islands / 14,439 boundary edges; Hero has 90 islands and a long cloth strap near the right hand that stretches during walk/attack. Automatic rigging alone is not production-ready.
- **Current verdicts:** Sunny core10 rig conditionally accepted (walk/boxing/death read; underarm pinch; needs optimization). Hero v3 raw is canonically better (no fused weapons) but rig needs retopo/weight cleanup. Mountain King v1 has excellent texture/face but only two functional arms; rejected for canon.
- **Never integrate a generated mesh before Blender weight/rig validation.** Keep P1 fallbacks. Native Mixamo clips can be used through a native AnimationPlayer, or retargeted; UAL does not bind directly to the 52-bone Mixamo skeleton.
- **Working files:** `incoming_models/P0_characters/`, manifest `docs/art/generated_asset_manifest.md`, canon prompts `docs/art/canon_asset_bible_2026-11-02.md`.

## 2026-11-02 РІР‚вЂќ Image-to-3D cast: integration checkpoint

- **User accepted all four concepts**, including the non-canon horns/face/limb-count choices; they are now the approved art direction for this pass.
- **Hero/Auro v6** (Image-to-3D from the accepted concept) is integrated via `Mannequin`'s native 52-bone AnimationPlayer branch; caravan/pilgrim/full-route tests 20/20.
- **Carapace Scavenger generated** (Image-to-3D, custom 38-bone rig, authored 5 clips) is integrated into `carapace_enemy.tscn`; carapace/arena tests 11/11.
- **Sunny real v2 core10** is accepted but not yet wired into `sunny_waking_poc`; campaign still uses the POC UAL model.
- **Mountain King and Carapace Centurion** Image-to-3D raws and custom rigs are accepted; automatic human-preset animations and rigid-island weights both produce plate/fur explosions. They are **not integrated**; fallback p3/procedural models remain. Next pass must be a manual retopo/rigid-part weight pass, not another generation.
- **Current generated files:** `incoming_models/P0_characters/`, `incoming_models/P1_monsters/`; manifest `docs/art/generated_asset_manifest.md`; previews `docs/art/previews/generated/`.

## 2026-11-02 - Character animation pass: v3 Sunny + AnimationTree

- **Real-world Sunny = generated v3, native 52-bone clips.** `sunny_waking_poc.tscn` uses `sunny_real_v3_clean2.glb`, not UAL. UALРІвЂ вЂ™v3 retarget was tested and rejected: shoulder holes and ballooned knees. The v3 model uses its native Mixamo-style clips.
- **v3 cleanup.** Fused sword vertices removed; `HumanRelaxedWalkLoopF` trimmed to `WalkClean` (0.8РІР‚вЂњ1.8 s) with Hips Y/Z drift removed; `HumanLandrollR` forward drift removed; MikkTSpace tangents and single-sided material added. Source `sunny_real_v3.glb` and clean `sunny_real_v3_clean.glb` remain as fallback.
- **AnimationTree is the runtime source of pose.** v3 and P1 Nightmare each have a 15-state state machine with a Locomotion blend space and a full manual cancellation matrix. `SunnyAnimator` uses the tree when assigned and falls back to direct `AnimationPlayer` for KayKit.
- **P1 Nightmare = 53-bone UAL fallback.** It uses the shared `sunny_animation_tree.tres`, `Jog_Fwd`/`Sprint`, a runtime `BoneAttachment3D` on `RightHand` with `short_sword.glb`, and `start_unarmed=true`. The sword is hidden until `CombatController.set_unarmed(false)`.
- **Attack timing is clip-driven.** Measured hand-speed peaks: P1 `Sword_Attack` 0.400 s, v3 `HumanSpBoxing` 0.400 s. Armed attack: cooldown 0.95, windup 0.30, active 0.20, contact 0.40. Unarmed P1: cooldown 0.90, same window. This replaces the previous 0.50 s attack that cut the clip at ~43 %.
- **Dodge is action-length, not clip-length.** Dodge duration 0.80 s, i-frames 0.30 s, speed 4.0; animator holds `Roll` for 0.80 s instead of the full 1.47 s. Landing holds are 0.80 s (P1) / 0.60 s (v3). Attack follow-through holds for 1.20 s.
- **Guard hit/break are real states.** `CombatController` calls `play_block_hit()` / `play_guard_break()`; the tree uses `GuardHit` (`Hit_Head`) and `GuardBreak` (`Hit_Chest`).
- **Foot grounding is visual-only.** `FootGrounder` moves the model mesh, never the `CharacterBody3D`; it is disabled for jump/fall/dodge/death. v3 idle feet measured 0.020 m; walk 0.03РІР‚вЂњ0.09 m; run flight 0.15РІР‚вЂњ0.18 m.
- **Enemy hit reactions are procedural for now.** Mountain King and Carapace Scavenger have short visual flinches; they do not have authored hit clips.
- **NPC native walk is cleaned.** `Mannequin` creates `WalkClean` for Hero v6 and uses `interact`/`pickup` poses.
- **Tests.** Full GUT 620/622; the two failures are an order-dependent act4 stealth test (passes alone) and a dialogue-bark case mismatch. Targeted animation/combat/route tests pass. Manual playthrough, 1080p/60 FPS and weapon-hitbox alignment remain NOT TESTED.

## 2026-11-02 - UI/UX demo pass: HUD, journal, map, pause

- **HUD has one source of truth for objectives.** `ObjectiveLine` prefers `QuestSystem` while `active_scope` is set (Labyrinth), otherwise `CampaignGuide` (First Nightmare). It does not merge two objectives into one line.
- **Labyrinth map is data, not a mock-up.** `resources/map/locations/labyrinth_first_fragment.tres` contains 18 zone polygons, the route, 17 markers, and `world_bounds = Rect2(-1470, -160, 1520, 320)`. `region_forgotten_shore` is ACTIVE and composes it through `MapPlacement`; map markers use real `NightmareRunState` flags and `hide_when_flag`.
- **Pause is a global autoload with explicit modal rules.** `PauseMenuSystem` opens only in a 3D scene with a player, refuses when PlayerMenuSystem/MapSystem/inventory/SoulSea is open, and restores pause/mouse exactly. It does not replace the game menu; it is a separate top layer.
- **Quest journal reads live data.** `RpgQuestScreen` uses `QuestSystem.get_journal_entries()` first, `CampaignGuide.history` second, and DEMO catalog only as a fallback when no game data exists. Tracking calls `QuestSystem.set_tracked_quest()`.
- **Dialogue UI stays in the lower third.** Subtitle panel accepts click/`advance_dialogue`, shows line counter, and does not pause the world. Bark panel remains separate.
- **UI scale is project-wide.** Viewport 1600x900, window override 1600x900, stretch `canvas_items`, aspect `expand`. Control/HUD sizes are authored against this reference; 1280x720 РІР‚В¦ 4K are scaled by the engine.
- **Not done in this pass:** real Labyrinth items/currency in the backpack, Memories screen with live data, final painted map art, gamepad remapping, localization. These remain backlog, not silent claims.
- **Audit/design:** `docs/demo_sprint/audits/ui_ux.md`, `docs/demo_sprint/design/ui_ux_design.md`.

## 2026-11-02 - Resource BOM fix and verification

- **Three `.tres` files had a UTF-8 BOM**, which Godot's text resource parser rejects with `Parse Error: Expected '['`: `resources/combat/attacks/sunny_light_armed.tres`, `sunny_light_unarmed_p1.tres`, `combat_settings.tres`. They were rewritten as UTF-8 **without** BOM; content unchanged. This removes the startup parse errors that prevented the MCP runtime helper from connecting.
- **Verification:** headless editor import exit 0 with no resource parse errors; targeted GUT `test_caravan_site`, `test_pilgrim_path`, `test_full_route`, `test_carapace_enemy`, `test_combat_arena` РІР‚вЂќ **36/36 tests, 1076 asserts, exit 0**; full GUT after the generated-NPC integration РІР‚вЂќ **636/636 tests, 7333 asserts, exit 0** (281.8 s).
- **Rule:** write `.tres`/`.tscn`/`.gd` with UTF-8 without BOM; do not use Windows PowerShell `Set-Content -Encoding UTF8` (it adds a BOM).

## 2026-11-03 - Generated NPC replacements for Shifty, Scholar, Veteran, BroadSlave

- **Generated NPCs replace the P1 caricatures for the four main camp roles.** `Mannequin` now resolves `shifty` РІвЂ вЂ™ `shifty_v4`, `scholar` РІвЂ вЂ™ `scholar_v3`, `veteran` РІвЂ вЂ™ `veteran_v5`, and the new `broad_slave` archetype РІвЂ вЂ™ `broad_slave_v3`. P1 models remain unchanged as fallbacks.
- **Native 52-bone clips, not UAL.** All four are Hyper3D rigs with `core` skinning and the same 10 Mixamo-style clips as the generated hero path. `Mannequin` builds `WalkClean` from `HumanRelaxedWalkLoopF`; no UAL retarget is injected.
- **Heights:** Shifty 0.93 РІвЂ вЂ™ 1.75 m, Scholar 0.98 РІвЂ вЂ™ 1.86 m, Veteran 0.95 РІвЂ вЂ™ 1.80 m, BroadSlave 0.975 РІвЂ вЂ™ 1.85 m. The generated GLB origin is centred; wrappers put the model at +0.949 m before `Mannequin` applies the height scale.
- **This is a visual upgrade, not final art.** The four NPCs are still semi-stylized; Scholar's coat needs weight cleanup, hands are soft, and none has LOD/optimized textures. Final photorealism still requires realistic concept sheets or scanned sources, as with Sunny v3.
- **Verification:** targeted GUT `test_caravan_site`, `test_pilgrim_path`, `test_full_route`, `test_carapace_enemy`, `test_combat_arena` РІР‚вЂќ **36/36, 1077 asserts, exit 0**; full GUT РІР‚вЂќ **636/636, 7333 asserts, exit 0**. Manual playthrough / foot contact / 1080p performance remain NOT TESTED.

## 2026-11-03 - Mountain King custom clips, Nightmare dress placeholder, horse blocker

- **Mountain King v2 is integrated.** The 60-bone image-to-3D custom rig now uses authored native clips (`Idle`, `Walk`, `Telegraph`, `Attack`, `Hit`, `Death`) instead of human presets. `mountain_king_gen_image_anim_v2.glb` is wrapped at Р“вЂ”2.37 and +2.25 m; `scenes/characters/mountain_king.tscn` points at it. The death clip is a safe hunched collapse, not a full ragdoll; arm/fur weights still need a manual pass.
- **Sunny Nightmare v2 is a dressed placeholder.** The image-to-3D body is rigged with 52 native bones, given brown/wool/leather materials, and integrated through a v3-style native AnimationTree and `WalkClean`. The silhouette is still a sweater/jeans; a real tunic/bare-foot mesh is not done.
- **White horse: clean stock source used.** The generated scan's tail is fused into the photogrammetry islands; the attempted tail rebuild and white texture are rejected. `caravan_site.tscn` now uses the Sketchfab **“Horse Rig”** by CreativeMachine (**CC BY 4.0**, UID `bd71883bad9845389ef4ec2801004801`), re-materialled white via `assets/characters/generated/white_horse_stock/`. It is static (no walk clips), with WhiteHorse/DarkHorse z-offset `-0.20`; the Quaternius placeholder remains fallback.
- **Centipede / worm rigs.** `centipede_gen_a_rigged.glb` has a 12-bone rigid-segment spine with `Idle`/`Walk`; `worm_knot_gen_b_rigged.glb` has a 7-bone spine with `Idle`/`Squirm`. These are asset-level rigs, not integrated enemy scenes.
- **Centurion / Blood Flower.** Centurion has a dark material variant preserving its 54-bone custom rig and five clips; Blood Flower has a 52-bone core rig with 10 native clips. No enemy scene/AI yet.
- **Verification:** targeted MK + Nightmare + horse + route tests — **46/46, 1125 asserts, exit 0**; full GUT — **636/636, 7333 asserts, exit 0** (after the stock-horse swap); targeted **46/46, 1125 asserts, exit 0**. Manual playthrough and 1080p remain NOT TESTED.

## 2026-11-03 — Переназначение управления

- **Пользовательские привязки лежат в `user://settings.cfg`.** `GameSettings` хранит в секции `input` только переопределённые действия как массивы словарей событий; действия без переопределения при каждом `apply_input()` восстанавливаются из `project.godot`. Так будущие изменения дефолтов не теряются, а файл не дублирует весь InputMap.
- **Дубликаты разрешены, конфликты показываются.** Часть действий намеренно делит клавиши (E — взаимодействие и следующая реплика, ЛКМ — удар и захват мыши), поэтому переназначение не стирает чужую привязку молча. UI предупреждает, какое действие уже использует клавишу; игрок сам решает, переназначить его или сбросить.
- **UI переназначения общий.** `InputBindingRow` используется и в главном меню, и в паузе. Esc отменяет ожидание, Backspace/Delete очищает, стрелка сброса возвращает одно действие, на странице есть общий сброс. `CONFIG_VERSION` поднят до 4.
- **Проверка:** целевые GUT `test_game_settings` 12/12, `test_input_binding_row` 6/6, `test_main_menu` 7/7, `test_ui_demo` 12/12; headless-запуск без ошибок скриптов. Полный прогон и ручная проверка переназначения в бою — **NOT TESTED**.

## 2026-11-03 - Coral vitals HUD

- **Layered HUD, one owner.** CombatHud remains the only HUD owner and the only subscriber to HealthComponent/StaminaComponent. A new VitalsBar Control inside CombatHud/PlayerPanel renders the coral reliquary; a pure VitalsDisplay RefCounted owns frame-rate independent smoothing, the delayed ghost bar and flash decay. HealthComponent is unchanged.
- **Art pipeline.** Hyper3D CLI generates two GLB reliefs (frame 442c7ad3-b6bf-4080-8d74-c4e2b6fcb8c3, medallion 8ae8e4d8-3993-4d8e-a827-6ec86dd7399a); Blender renders them orthographically with alpha; tools/art/finalize_vitals_sprites.py trims, recolors coral to dark crimson and resizes. Fill/plate/glow/shine/notch are project-owned procedural PNGs. The fill is grayscale and tinted at runtime by health state.
- **Effects are separated.** The low-health vignette is a full-screen ColorRect shader child of CombatHud, below Root; it is not part of the bar. Damage flash, medallion pulse, ghost trail and status text are separate layers.
- **Verification.** Headless import exit 0; test_vitals_hud 6/6; test_combat_hud 2/2, 6 asserts; full GUT 656/656 tests, 7,424 asserts, output reports All tests passed; 1600x900 capture produced full/wounded/critical frames. Manual gameplay and 1080p performance are NOT TESTED.

## 2026-11-03 - Equipment alpha: shared skeleton, full-set armour

- **Full-set body armour, not a paper doll.** The alpha uses one `BODY` slot and one skinned `ArmorSetData`; it deliberately does not split chest/bracers/greaves. `docs/design/12_inventory_economy_and_consumables.md` §6.1/§6.8 already defines one armour Memory and no set bonuses/sockets.
- **Shared skeleton, not pose copying.** `EquipmentComponent` reparents the armour `MeshInstance3D` under the base `Skeleton3D`, remaps the `Skin` by bone name, and points `mesh.skeleton` at the base skeleton. No second skeleton and no second `AnimationPlayer`.
- **Nightmare v2 is the alpha base.** Its generated mesh has four material surfaces; the armour shell duplicates tunic/trousers/shoes, inflates them and replaces those surfaces. `sunny_real_v3` remains out of scope until a base-body split exists.
- **Weapon/accessory sockets.** `BoneAttachment3D` is created on demand from `CharacterRigProfile.socket_bones` (`hand_r` -> `RightHand`, `hips` -> `Hips`). Existing `short_sword.glb` and `water_flask.glb` are the alpha props.
- **Isolated validation.** `scenes/dev/equipment_lab.tscn` and `scenes/dev/sunny_equipment_preview.tscn` are dev-only; campaign scenes are unchanged. GUT `test_equipment_alpha` is 11/11, 46 asserts, exit 0; full GUT in demo/integration 683/683, 7676 asserts, exit 0; headless import exit 0. Manual playthrough/1080p are NOT TESTED.
- **Alpha limitations.** The set is a blockout; the v3 attack clip is `HumanSpBoxing`, so a drawn sword does not have a sword attack animation; save/UI integration is still pending.

## 2026-11-03 - Placeholder replacement pilot: stealth covers

- **Pilot scope.** The three visible `CSGBox3D` covers in `pilgrim_path_art.tscn` (`StealthSection/Covers/CoverWest`, `CoverEast`, `CoverEdge`) are replaced by three Hyper3D-generated dark rock GLBs (`assets/environments/nightmare/path/stealth_cover_{a,b,c}.glb`). No other location geometry is changed in this pass.
- **Visuals vs collision are separated.** The new meshes live under `Art/StealthCovers` at the exact original transforms. The original CSG nodes are hidden (`visible=false`) but keep `use_collision=true`; this preserves the route and collision footprint exactly and keeps the art layer visual-only. The hidden CSG still blocks (collision probe: 0 failures).
- **Material.** The GLBs use the `nm_rock` slot; their `.import` maps it to `nm_rock_cover.tres`, a project-owned variant of `nm_rock.tres` with a darker base tint and `snow_coverage=0.0` so the covers read as wind-scoured rock instead of disappearing into the snow.
- **Pipeline.** Hyper3D CLI `Gen-2.5-High`, Quad, GLB, PBR; Blender 5.1 post-processing via `assets/environments/nightmare/source/build_stealth_covers.py` (join, target scale, bottom-centre origin, decimate to ~3.2k/2.6k tris, UV box, normals). Raw inputs stay gitignored in `incoming_models/P2_environment/stealth_covers/`.
- **Verification.** `test_pilgrim_path` 10/10 (260 asserts), `test_full_route` 3/3 (220 asserts), collision probe failures=0; before/after captures in `docs/art/previews/nightmare/stealth_covers_*.jpg`. Manual playthrough and performance are NOT TESTED.
- **Next batch.** `Z02_Crab*` in the labyrinth was the highest-value remaining placeholder: `LabyrinthArt._replace_carcass_placeholders()` only hid direct children, so nested crab meshes stayed visible. **Fixed 2026-11-03:** recursive `_hide_carcass_placeholders()` now hides the `Z02_Crab0/1/2` subtrees; runtime audit reports 0 `Z02_Crab*` meshes and `test_labyrinth_first_fragment` is 14/14 with a regression test. Next: containment corridor / observation post — **done 2026-11-03**, see the entry at the end of this file.

## 2026-11-03 - Systems audit snapshot

- **Status.** Full GUT green on rerun: 88 scripts / 711 tests / 7 811 asserts / exit 0. Script audit 385/0, resource audit 715/0, headless import exit 0. Main menu and campaign entry boot headless. One earlier full run aborted at `test_caravan_march` with exit -1; isolated test 7/7, rerun green.
- **Release gate is still blocked.** No `export_presets.cfg`, `MCPRuntime` is still an autoload, and the full manual S00-S15 / post-nightmare pass is NOT TESTED.
- **State split is the main integration debt.** Campaign state is `NightmareRunState`; the equipment alpha uses `InventoryComponent`/`EquipmentComponent`, and `SaveSystem` snapshots only the former. The two loot layers are intentional per `DECISIONS.md`; equipment ID persistence is implemented in NightmareRunState; demo-inventory save/load is **NOT TESTED**.
- **Modal ownership is still per-system.** `MapSystem`, `PlayerMenuSystem`, `PauseMenuSystem`, `SoulSeaController` and the inventory prototype each own pause/mouse/group exclusion. The `GameModes` stack from `docs/design/13_systems_architecture_audit.md` is not adopted; do this before adding more modal screens.
- **Known visual/content gaps.** `LabyrinthArt` nested `Z02_Crab*` hide is fixed (0 visible, 14/14 labyrinth tests with a regression test). SQ-02 (`echo_polyp`), SQ-04 (tide lock) and SQ-05 (Old Shell) still wait on missing content. README was updated 2026-11-03 (current GUT counts, labyrinth status, audio, equipment alpha).
- **Detailed report:** `docs/production/implemented_systems_audit_2026-11-03.md`.

## 2026-11-03 - World framing pass: distant horizons and travelled sky

- **WorldFrame kit.** `scripts/world/world_frame.gd` plus `assets/shaders/world_frame_*.gdshader` provide visual-only ridge rings, far-sea planes, reef bands, city skylines, spire clusters, water fins and mass blocks. They are unshaded and fog-disabled with baked haze, create no collision, and are used by the labyrinth, Black Sea, tide descent, academy approach/gate/campus, ambush site and the coral sandbox.
- **Sky as a travelled gradient.** `LabyrinthAtmosphere` now blends sky top/horizon/cloud colour, sun energy, fog colour/density and ambient by active-camera X (pale eastern day -> neutral centre -> dark western storm). Authored labyrinth shot cameras were raised to a 4000 m far plane so the horizon is visible in captures and cutscenes.
- **Fog retuned, not removed.** `env_labyrinth` depth fog moved from 70-420 m / 0.40 to 90-700 m / 0.32 so the mid-ground and ridge ring read; the west storm still raises density to 0.62-0.85.
- **No gameplay changes.** Spawns, portals, markers, collision, tide gates and routes are untouched. New geometry is visual-only and sits outside the playable corridor.
- **Verification:** targeted GUT `test_labyrinth_first_fragment` 13/13 (334 asserts), `test_post_nightmare_locations` 15/15 (516 asserts), `test_ambush_site` 16/16 (93 asserts), `test_world_frame` 6/6 (39 asserts); read-only captures for the labyrinth (low/high), Black Sea, tide descent, academy approach/gate/campus and ambush site. The system audit (`docs/art/world_framing_system_audit.md`) measured the kit at +8 draw calls after the MultiMesh spire conversion (was +30), 0 collision, 0 lights, rebuild 15->15, and 1080p 135.8 FPS / 1579 draw calls / 795.4 MB VRAM. Manual 1.5 km playthrough remains NOT TESTED.

## 2026-11-03 — Generic loot pools, tide-wrack caches, GD containers

- **Two loot layers, on purpose.** The untracked equipment-only prototype (`LootTable`, `LootEntry`, `LootDropComponent`, `WorldLootItem`, `InventoryComponent`, `scenes/dev/equipment_loot_demo.tscn`) is left intact. The labyrinth uses a new generic layer (`LootPool`, `LootPoolEntry`, `LootItemData`, `LootCatalog`, `LootPoolRoller`, `LootCache`, `LootDropper`) because the run inventory is `NightmareRunState` and includes currency, materials, consumables, ordinary equipment and Memories, not only `EquipmentItemData`.
- **Canon vs adaptation.** `docs/design/forgotten_shore_labyrinth_research.md` §5.4 forbids generic chests, clothed skeletons and notes in the first fragment. The user requested interactive containers, so the placed caches are marked GD: tide-wrack strongboxes/barrels and a coral ossuary/sarcophagus. There are no notes, no clothed corpses and no shrine/altar. If strict canon is required, remove the three sarcophagi and keep the four chests + three barrel clusters.
- **No humanoid bandits in the current fragment.** `enemy_humanoid_bandit` is `GD_FUTURE` and filtered by default. It models gear-based drops with `worn/used/fine` tiers and never drops Memories (canon ch. 35: Memories are destroyed with their owner).
- **No recipes or crafting.** Unique boss rewards are Memories (`starlight_legion_armor`, `midnight_shard`, `drop_of_ichor`), not relic recipes. Reef Warden set pieces are `GD_FUTURE` trophies; no set bonuses or equipment slots yet.
- **One-shot and deterministic.** Each cache writes `loot_<id>_opened` to `NightmareRunState`; enemy drops write `loot_<tag>_dropped`. RNG is seeded by container id / pool id + spawn position, so reload does not reroll.
- **Placement is data, not code.** Ten `LootCache` instances live under `Gameplay/Interactables/Secrets` in `labyrinth_first_fragment.tscn`; their pools are `.tres` in `resources/loot/tables/`. Enemy drops are attached in `LabyrinthQuestDirector._spawn_enemy()` through `LootDropper`, without changing `CarapaceEnemy` AI.
- **Verification.** `tools/audit/loot_pool_test.gd` — CHEST/DROPPER/FUTURE_FILTER/SCAVENGER all OK, failures=0; `tools/audit/loot_secret_audit.gd` — 10/10 caches on ground, delta 0.00, overlaps 0; headless import exit 0. Manual playthrough, live E interaction and save/load round-trip are NOT TESTED.

## 2026-11-03 — Armor stats, generated sets and equipment loot

- **Armor is a data item, not a visual toggle.** `ArmorItem` extends `ArmorSetData` and adds `ArmorStats`; `CharacterStats` is the only aggregator. It turns armor into `HealthComponent.damage_reduction`, impact into `MeleeAttack.damage_bonus`, weight into an encumbrance speed penalty, stagger resistance into guard-break duration, and max-health modifiers into `HealthComponent.configure()`. Unequipping recomputes from base values; no permanent character fields are overwritten.
- **Damage types are passed through the hit chain.** `AttackStats.damage_type` -> `MeleeAttack.apply_hit()` -> `Hurtbox.receive_hit()` -> `HealthComponent.take_damage()`. Existing resources default to `physical`; the existing enemy/player attacks now declare slash/pierce/blunt so armor resistances are meaningful.
- **Weapon stow moves the same visual.** `WeaponData.sheath_socket` (hips/back) is used by `EquipmentComponent.set_weapon_drawn()`; the weapon is reparented between `hand_r` and the sheath socket instead of being duplicated or simply hidden.
- **Generated armour is re-bound, not skin-remapped.** The Hyper3D human template has different bone rest lengths from `sunny_nightmare_v2`; a plain `Skin` remap compressed the outfit. `rebind_generated_armor.py` transfers the base body's vertex weights to the armour, limits to four influences, binds it to the base armature, and exports a new GLB. `prepare_generated_armor.py` removes the blank mannequin head island first.
- **Three generated sets are registered.** `scout_leather`, `mercenary_medium`, `heavy_plate` use `ArmorItem` resources and the `generated_v3` 52-bone profile. Scout keeps the base head (`hide_surfaces = [1,2,3]`); mercenary/heavy hide it (`[0,1,2,3]`) because their helmets are closed.
- **Two loot layers remain separate.** The campaign's generic `LootPool`/`LootCache`/`LootDropper` path writes currency/materials/Memories to `NightmareRunState`. The equipment layer (`LootTable`, `LootDropComponent`, `WorldLootItem`, `LootContainer`, `InventoryComponent`) spawns physical equipment drops and falls back to `NightmareRunState.add_item()` when no demo inventory exists. The layers are not merged.
- **Verification.** Headless import exit 0; `test_armor_stats_loot` 19/19 (81 asserts); `test_equipment_alpha` 11/11 (46 asserts); `test_combat_arena` 6/6 (19 asserts); `test_carapace_enemy` 10/10 (45 asserts); full GUT 711/711, 7,811 asserts, exit 0. 1600x900 captures in `docs/art/previews/equipment/`. Manual playthrough and 1080p/60 FPS are **NOT TESTED**; equipment ID persistence is covered by `test_equipment_ids_persist_in_run_state`.

## 2026-11-03 — Loot audit fixes: LootBag, collisions, open-state

- **Enemy loot is manual.** `LootDropper` no longer calls `LootPoolRoller.apply()` on death. It rolls the pool, sets `loot_<tag>_dropped` (bag created), and spawns `scenes/loot/loot_bag.tscn` (`LootBag`) at the corpse. `LootBag.interact()` grants the drops and shows the toast. If the bag is not picked up before death/reload, it is lost; the enemy does not drop a second bag. This matches `12_inventory_economy_and_consumables.md` §0.1/§13.4.
- **Containers are solid.** `chest_wood_iron.tscn`, `loot_barrel_cluster.tscn`, `ancient_tomb_sarcophagus.tscn` now have `StaticBody3D` + box `CollisionShape3D` (layer 1, mask 0). `loot_secret_audit` excludes the cache's own body from ray/overlap queries.
- **Open-state feedback and lid animation.** The chest lid and sarcophagus plate were split in Blender into `Body`/`Lid` with the origin at the back hinge. `LootCache` animates `Lid` around X (chest 100°, sarcophagus 75°); opening also shows a `Label3D` "Обыскано" and `impact_dust`.
- **Glow moved out of the mesh.** Omni light heights: chest 1.15 m, barrels 1.05 m, sarcophagus 1.85 m.
- **Relic pools split; unique enforced; enemy drop seed stable.** `cache_ossuary_relic` was split into helm/plate pools with guaranteed pieces; `LootPoolRoller.apply()` skips already-owned unique items; `LootDropper.drop_seed` is set from `enemy_tag`.
- **Verification.** `loot_pool_test` — CHEST (incl. label), DROPPER (no auto-grant, bag pickup), FUTURE_FILTER, UNIQUE, SCAVENGER — `failures=0`; `loot_save_roundtrip_test` OK; `loot_data_audit` 27/11/10, `failures=0`; `loot_secret_audit` 10/10; headless import exit 0. Full GUT in the current tree: 717/717, 7849 asserts, exit 0. Manual playthrough, live E, and 1080p remain NOT TESTED.

## 2026-11-03 — Equipment/loot audit fixes

- **Health max changes emit one correct signal.** `HealthComponent.set_max_health_preserving_ratio()` preserves the current-health ratio and emits `health_changed(current, max)` after both values are updated. `CharacterStats` uses it instead of `configure()` + direct `current_health` assignment; the HUD no longer receives stale current HP.
- **Inventory load is atomic.** `InventoryComponent.from_ids()` validates all IDs and capacity before clearing, snapshots the old state, and restores it if an add unexpectedly fails. A corrupted save no longer silently deletes the previous inventory.
- **`apply_ids()` is a full equipment entry point.** It validates body/weapon/accessories and checks `rig_profile` before replacing the loadout, matching `equip()`. A catalog entry for another skeleton is rejected instead of producing a broken visual.
- **Armour is excluded from body LOD toggling.** Attached armour meshes are added to `lod_ignore`; `CharacterLodVisibility` no longer risks hiding them at LOD1/2. Armour still has no LODs of its own — that remains a performance backlog item.
- **GUT order issue documented.** Loading the equipment demo before `test_side_quests` could invalidate the `-s`-mode global class cache for `QuestLever`. `test_armor_stats_loot.gd` preloads `quest_lever.gd` as a test-harness workaround; this is not a runtime fix.
- **Audit result.** Full GUT 711/711, 7,811 asserts, exit 0; `test_armor_stats_loot` 19/19, 81 asserts; headless import exit 0. GLB audit: 52 joints, 0 vertices with zero weights, weight sums 1.0, max 4 influences, tangents/UVs present. Material audit: PBR maps wired, opaque, double-sided. Residual risks: no armour LODs, no pose/death clipping test, no demo-inventory save/load round-trip, manual playthrough and 1080p/60 FPS **NOT TESTED**. Report: `docs/equipment_armor_loot_audit.md`.
- **Post-audit hardening.** Equipment IDs now travel through `NightmareRunState.snapshot()/restore()` and `EquipmentComponent.persist_to_run_state`; `LootDropComponent` passes an explicit `inventory_path` to spawned loot; `LootTable` uses strict `<` for chance and validates `max_drops`; `WeaponData` allows an empty sheath when `visible_when_sheathed=false`, and weapon validation accepts `grip_socket`; `set_weapon_drawn()` only emits when a weapon exists. Covered by `test_equipment_ids_persist_in_run_state`, `test_world_loot_uses_explicit_inventory_path`, `test_weapon_without_sheath_is_allowed`, `test_health_damage_type_resistance`, `test_inventory_ids_round_trip`, `test_loot_container_rejects_when_full`, `test_loot_drop_component_spawns_on_death`.

## 2026-11-03 — Loot UI data: icons, runtime catalog, stack limits, bag persistence

- **Category icons.** Eight procedural 128×128 RGBA PNGs live in `assets/ui/loot/icons/` and are generated by `tools/art/make_loot_icons.py` (Pillow). `LootItemData.icon` is assigned by category in `tools/build_loot_resources.gd`; `LootBag` shows the first drop's icon via a `Sprite3D`.
- **Runtime catalog.** `LootRegistry` loads `resources/loot/loot_catalog.tres` once and is used by bag deserialization, stack limits and icons.
- **Stack limits.** `LootPoolRoller.apply()` returns the part that did not fit `stack_max`. `LootBag` keeps the remainder and can be interacted with again after the player frees space; `LootCache` spawns an overflow `LootBag`. Nothing is silently deleted.
- **Bag persistence.** `NightmareRunState` stores `loot_bags` (bag id, scene, position, serialized drops); `LootBagPersistence` in the labyrinth scene respawns unpicked bags after `restore()`/scene load. `snapshot`/`restore` is tested; the file `SaveSystem` with bags is NOT TESTED.
- **Lid animation.** Chest lid and sarcophagus plate were split in Blender into `Body`/`Lid` with the origin at the back hinge. `LootCache` animates `Lid` around X: chest 100°, sarcophagus 75°.
- **Route/boundary.** `route_reachability.gd` now includes `LootCache`: 18/18 zones, 61/61 POIs (all 10 secrets PASS). The boundary audit does not flag any `Secret_*`/`Loot*`; the pre-existing 3,482 lethal edges, 2 lethal jumps, 2 ghost floors (Z10) and 61 floating objects remain level-design debt.
- **Verification.** `loot_pool_test` — CHEST (lid 100°), SARCOPHAGUS (75°), DROPPER (bag + icon), BAG_PERSIST, STACK_LIMIT, CACHE_OVERFLOW, FUTURE_FILTER, UNIQUE, SCAVENGER — `failures=0`; `loot_save_roundtrip_test` OK; `loot_data_audit` 27/11/10, `failures=0`; `loot_secret_audit` 10/10; headless import exit 0; full GUT **717/717, 7849 asserts, exit 0**. ## 2026-11-03 - Reference-layout HUD pass

- **Composition follows the reference.** Player vitals moved to the top-left; enemy health to the top-centre; minimap and quest tracker to the top-right; tide below the quest tracker; quick items to the bottom-left; control hints and quest toast to the bottom-right; the interaction prompt sits under the vitals. The subtitle panel remains bottom-centre. Full coordinates: `docs/design/hud_reference_analysis.md`.
- **Minimap is a view, not a second map system.** `HudMinimap` reads `MapSystem.minimap_snapshot()` and draws the current `MapDefinition.features` plus visible `MapMarker`s inside a circular `SubViewport`. North is up; the player arrow follows the existing forward vector. `MapSystem` and the map atlas remain the only sources of map data.
- **Quick items are a read-only carried-supplies strip.** `HudQuickItems` reads `NightmareRunState.get_item_count()` for `provisions`, `water_skin` and `torch`, and refreshes on `item_changed`. It does not create a second inventory or invent quantities.
- **Control hints are live.** `HudControlHints` reads the first event of `sprint`, `interact` and `open_map` from `InputMap`; rebinding the action updates the keycap instead of leaving a stale hint.
- **Quest tracker.** `ObjectiveLine` is now right-aligned and responsive to its 336 px column; for an active `QuestSystem` quest the caption uses the quest title (e.g. «СИРЕНЬ И КРЫЛОБЫК») and the objective sits beneath it.
- **Not ported.** The recording FPS overlay and video progress bar are external UI. Time-of-day/weather icons are not invented: the project has a tide system, and `TideIndicator` remains the authoritative environment readout. The Witcher wolf medallion is replaced by the project's coral reliquary; no Witcher asset is copied.
- **Verification.** `test_hud_reference` 5/5, 31 asserts; targeted HUD/combat suite 48/48, 237 asserts; full GUT 89 scripts, 717/717 tests, 7,849 asserts, exit 0; 1600×900 captures `hud_reference_full.png` and `hud_reference_subtitles.png`. Manual gameplay, 1080p/60 FPS, gamepad and 4:3 are **NOT TESTED**.

## 2026-11-03 — Armour LOD, cloth/plate split, pose audit, inventory persistence

- **LOD is per-item, one GLB.** Each generated armour GLB contains `model_LOD0/1/2` skinned to the same 52-bone armature. `EquipmentComponent` drives `visibility_range_begin/end` (0-8 m, 8-18 m, 18 m+) with 0.5 m margins; no second skeleton, AnimationPlayer or per-frame LOD script.
- **Cloth and plate are separate materials.** `split_armor_materials.py` classifies faces by the metallic channel of the metallic-roughness map: plate faces get a single-sided material, cloth keeps double-sided. Heavy is all plate; Scout/Mercenary keep both. This removes overdraw on closed plate without opening holes in hoods/cloaks/tabards.
- **Scout hood is rigid to Head.** `fix_armor_head_weights.py` assigns the hood/helmet region (sphere around the Head bone, z margin 0.08, radius 0.24) to 100% `Head`. Head turn is now clean. A full rigid-plate pass (`rigidify_armor_plates.py`) was evaluated and rejected: it separated plates in the boxing guard; smooth transferred weights remain for the body shell.
- **Attack clipping is animation content, not a weight bug.** `HumanSpBoxing` is the only attack clip in the generated Nightmare v2 set; its boxing guard drives large pauldrons/cloak into the head. Pose captures are retained as evidence. A sword-swing clip or separated pauldron meshes is the required fix.
- **Demo inventory stacks persist.** `NightmareRunState` has a separate `inventory` map (id -> count) in snapshot/restore; `InventoryComponent.persist_to_run_state` saves/restores stacks through the catalog. Campaign `items` and equipment `inventory` stay separate.
- **Verification.** `test_armor_stats_loot` 20/20, 88 asserts; full GUT 717/717, 7,849 asserts, exit 0; headless import exit 0. LOD capture at 2/10/20 m and pose captures at 1600x900. Manual playthrough, 1080p/60 FPS and cloth simulation remain **NOT TESTED**.

## 2026-11-03 - Stage 5 integration: Academy, cutscenes, rebind

- **Academy is a first-class playable segment.** `academy_grounds_playable.tscn` and `academy_sleeper_center_playable.tscn` are reachable from the main menu via «Академия · ALPHA». The segment keeps its own `academy` quest scope, 24 quests (12 MQ + 12 SQ), 23 NPC, 23 interactables and 11 loot caches. It does not alter the M1 route; P05–B02 remain the canonical M1 path.
- **Academy wrappers own the gameplay layer.** Art scenes stay clean; `AcademyGameplayDirector` spawns NPC/interactables/triggers/loot/portals, opens the red gate on `academy_gate_passed`, and filters quests by scope. This keeps geometry and quest content independently editable.
- **Cutscene framework is live.** `CutsceneDirector` is an autoload with one lifecycle owner: camera shots, letterbox/subtitles, input/combat lock, skip policy, completion flags and actor restore. M1 now uses real cutscenes for P05 wake/mirror, P09 induction/capsule and B01 shroud; dialogue fallback remains if a stage is missing. `PauseMenuSystem`, `PlayerMenuSystem` and `MapSystem` check `is_locked()` and do not open modals during a cutscene.
- **Skip is completion-equivalent.** Skipping or aborting restores actors/props/camera/HUD and applies declared `completion_flags`; it is not a timer jump. `once_flag` is bound to the story flag (e.g. `p05_jett_talked`), so reload/save cannot replay a finished scene.
- **Input rebinding is shared by main menu and pause.** `GameSettings` stores only overridden actions in `user://settings.cfg` section `input`; actions without overrides restore `project.godot` defaults. `InputBindingRow` is the single UI row for keyboard, mouse and gamepad; Backspace clears, Esc cancels.
- **Gamepad defaults are additive, not overriding.** Default stick/buttons are appended only when an action has no user override, so a custom binding is never silently extended. `cutscene_skip` remains owned by `CutsceneDirector`.
- **Authored enemy clips are registered.** `carapace_hit.tres`, `larva_hit.tres` and `larva_death.tres` are added to the default AnimationLibrary at runtime; procedural flinch/death remains the fallback when a clip is absent.
- **Verification.** Boot exit 0; full GUT **92 scripts / 749 tests / 749 pass / 8 039 asserts / exit 0**; `test_academy_segment` 5/5, `test_cutscene_integration` 4/4, `test_combat_ai_integration` 11/11, `test_post_nightmare_locations` 16/16, `test_labyrinth_first_fragment` 14/14; Academy probes 0 failures, walkthrough 24/24; route reachability 61/61; boundary 0 lethal, 2 residual Z10 art ghost cells. Temp release export succeeded (exe 109,268,480 B, PCK 1,000,473,040 B) and boots headless with exit 0; it still includes dev tools/MCPRuntime. Manual M1/tech/Academy playthrough and 1080p/60 remain **NOT TESTED**.
